# Piece P validation

## Round 8 (current)

2026-11-12 UTC. Checked HEAD: `08f9d88ce53cceca31216caf418315b8e3609735`.
Writer changes are uncommitted for the harness; exact final-commit qualification remains the
harness's responsibility. No pushes, review tools or review notes were made. ISS-0054 stays open.

Reconciliation now covers authored declarations and standalone command progress units, with
position-free command targets, disjoint replacing populations and same-piece snapshot ownership.
Both replacement and retirement require a reason and a structured, resolvable accepting review
reference at an ancestor commit. Retirement additionally checks that the original units are absent.
The denominator is unchanged, and replacement/retirement are reported separately from equality.

F01 is one property: complete root import reachability and production exact-import checks of
every HEAD proof file, using isolated freshly built modules (including probe/built dependency
agreement). The recorded F01 revision must be an actual ancestor. Spec CI retains history for
ancestry checks; it does not rebuild historical progress revisions. Controls use actual fixture
commits, including an unrelated root commit for ancestry refusal. Frozen header bytes are removed.

The coordinator's tag-creation, push and remote-verification procedure is documented in README.md.
Pin controls require diagnostics to include all absent tags in one run, while preserving bounded
fetches and refusal of changed local/remote pins.

Validation result: PASS (exit 0), including every pin, relocation and progress control and
the fresh production inventory census. F01 reports `landed=1/1`; F02 remains `0/5` with
two examples, pending actual piece implementation and reviewed reconciliation. The command was:

```sh
nix develop -c bash scripts/spec/landing_inventory.sh
```

Additional validation passed (exit 0):

```sh
nix develop -c bash scripts/checks/lib/bounded.sh 600 python3 scripts/proofs/imports.py
```

This ran the production checker on all twelve current proof modules and its declaring-owner,
extra/transitive-only import, anonymous-example, commented-header and rewrite controls.

Earlier development runs caught and corrected the F01 fixture's header syntax and an invalid-header
error path. The final controls include unused versus legitimate later imports, incomplete roots,
nonancestor provenance, command replacement, declaration/command retirement with absence checks,
foreign/shared targets, unresolvable review artifacts, and existing digest/relocation/pin controls.

## Round 7 (historical)

Round 7, 2026-11-12 UTC. Merged `origin/main` first, including F01. The checked HEAD is
`6d19d4489e6474bfe3464a4b9fd91ec9cee2e246`; writer changes remain uncommitted for the harness.
No push, review tool or review note was made. ISS-0054 remains open for independent review and
harness qualification of its final commit. The working-tree code bundle is hashed below.

Command progress and BASE membership share one content-and-scope sequence matcher: file,
enclosing namespace/section sequence, kind, source digest and ordinal among equal commands.
Parsed positions order the sequence but never enter matching keys. Snapshot positional names
remain audit keys. Controls cover identical commands in distinct scopes, duplicate multiplicity,
shifted imports/declarations before an unchanged example, and a changed example that does not land.
Generated constants remain excluded from progress; defining source, documentation and attributes
retain their full-digest checks.

F01 measures the twelve reviewed exact proof import headers in `headers.json`, with revision
provenance, text, byte extents and digests. Its population is checked against the pinned baseline.
The gate parses the stored headers without needing the historical F01 object in shallow CI.
Controls accept exact headers with an unavailable historical object, refuse an arbitrary
hash-consistent prefix extent, and stop counting changed imports. Current main reports
**F01 landed=12/12, remaining=0**.

Reviewed additive `replacement` rows now implement many-to-many declaration reconciliation.
The judgment preserves snapshot rows, validates the closed schema and accepting review reference,
and refuses unknown or already-replaced rows, generated constants, cross-piece/BASE obligations,
owner holds, malformed digests, extra/duplicate fields and rejected reviews. Every original unit
closes only when all recorded replacement declarations exist at HEAD with exact full digests.
Production-census controls cover renamed repairs, missing/changed replacements and partial closure.
No replacement rows have been added to the snapshot inventory yet. `--head-digests` exports the
checked digests so the next spec piece can prepare its reviewed reconciliation.

Relocation `reason` arrays use only checked cause kinds. Law/control membership, support and
dependency claims resolve against the actual group and dependency graph; fixture kinds resolve
against the shared-fixture list; regeneration and auxiliary kinds resolve against the census-backed
emissions. Generation prunes 75 inherited unrelated pair annotations, leaving every moved row
with a justified cause. Read-mode controls refuse fabricated fixtures, unrelated real pairs,
unobserved regeneration and unsupported auxiliary dependencies. All relocation records and the
table below are recomputed and compared. Plan assignments and owner decisions are unchanged.

Validation commands (all exited **0**, run under Nix):

```sh
nix develop -c bash scripts/spec/landing_inventory.sh --write --remaining F01 --head-digests /tmp/round7-head-digests.json
nix develop -c bash scripts/checks/root_inventory.sh
nix develop -c python3 /tmp/round7_artifact_checks.py
```

The inventory command includes every pin, relocation, progress, census, coverage, scheduling,
dependency, disposition, hold and replacement control. After writing, it rereads and judges the
persisted inventory against the same fresh census and checks relocation evidence in read mode.
The artifact checks verify TSV row widths, reviewed header digests, closed relocation kinds,
absence of an interrupted Git operation and `git diff --check`. Full legacy gates were not run.
